      </ul></div></div></div><div className="overflow-hidden border-x border-t sm:border-b sm:rounded-14 border-body-stroke-lighter last:border-b last:rounded-b-14"><button aria-label="One of my dependencies is a private package. Can I still use ToDesktop for Electron?" className="text-14/22 transition-all rounded-inherit font-500 pl-23 pt-23 pr-15 tracking-[-0.14px] text-start text-body-light-loud select-none cursor-pointer flex w-full justify-between pb-23"><span className="flex-1 block">One of my dependencies is a private package. Can I still use ToDesktop for Electron?</span>< /></button><div className="transition-all overflow-hidden relative pr-16 pl-23 h-0"><div className="text-body-normal text-body-light-muted transition-all opacity-0 blur-[1px]"><p>Absolutely. ToDesktop CLI is similar to Continuous Integration service so you can use the <a class='underline' href='https://docs.npmjs.com/using-private-packages-in-a-ci-cd-workflow/'>guide from npm.</a></p>
      <p class='mb-8'>To summarize:</p>
      <ol class='list-decimal list-outside ml-24 flex flex-col m-8 gap-8'>
        <li class='m-0 p-0'>Create a token using npm: npm token create --read-only.</li>
        <li class='m-0 p-0'>In ToDesktop's web UI go to Settings -> Build and Deploy.</li>
        <li class='m-0 p-0'>Enter an environment variable key of NPM_TOKEN and value should be the token entered above.</li>
        <li class='m-0 p-0'>Create an <code class='bg-body-light-bg-ghost rounded-6 p-4'>.npmrc</code> file in the root of your project with the following contents:</li>
      </ol>
      <pre class='bg-body-light-bg-ghost rounded-6 p-4 mb-12'><code class=''>//registry.npmjs.org/:_authToken=${NPM_TOKEN}</code></pre>
      <p>Note: <b>Do not put a token in this file</b>. You are specifying a literal value of <code class='bg-body-light-bg-ghost rounded-6 p-4'>{NPM_TOKEN}</code>. NPM will replace the value for you. 5. Add <code class='bg-body-light-bg-ghost rounded-6 p-4'>.npmrc</code> to your appFiles array <code class='bg-body-light-bg-ghost rounded-6 p-4'>[".npmrc"]</code> in <code class='bg-body-light-bg-ghost rounded-6 p-4'>todesktop.json</code>.</p></div></div></div><div className="overflow-hidden border-x border-t sm:border-b sm:rounded-14 border-body-stroke-lighter last:border-b last:rounded-b-14"><button aria-label="Can I migrate away from ToDesktop if it doesn&#39;t meet my needs?" className="text-14/22 transition-all rounded-inherit font-500 pl-23 pt-23 pr-15 tracking-[-0.14px] text-start text-body-light-loud select-none cursor-pointer flex w-full justify-between pb-23"><span className="flex-1 block">Can I migrate away from ToDesktop if it doesn&#39;t meet my needs?</span>< /></button><div className="transition-all overflow-hidden relative pr-16 pl-23 h-0"><div className="text-body-normal text-body-light-muted transition-all opacity-0 blur-[1px]"><p>Yes, we never want you to be stuck with a service that doesn't meet your needs.
